          o;a; wdodk úê                                        ia:dkSh l%uh

          Direct method
                                                               Data is entered directly on the system where it
          Rcq l%uh
                                                               will be used or processed.
          Methods by which data are captured and               o;a; iDcqju th Ndú;d lrk fyda ielfik
